1398
A data-driven framework called Generative Adversarial Privacy and Fairness (GAPF) is presented, which uses adversarial learning to create private and fair data representations by decoupling sensitive attributes from the rest of the dataset.
-----
-----
453
Researchers used automatic search techniques to discover a new activation function, Swish (f(x) = x * sigmoid(beta * x), which outperforms the widely-used Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) in deeper models on challenging datasets, improving accuracy by up to 0.9% on ImageNet.
-----
-----
351
Researchers proposed the Information Maximising Autoencoder (InfoAE), a model that learns disentangled representations from unlabelled data by maximizing the mutual information with given information, achieving 98.9% test accuracy on the MNIST dataset.
-----
-----
991
A novel algorithm, Deep Temporal Clustering (DTC), is proposed for unsupervised time series data analysis, combining dimensionality reduction and clustering into a single end-to-end framework, outperforming traditional methods in diverse domains.
-----
-----
2029
Researchers improved a reinforcement learning algorithm, Q-learning with UCB-exploration, for infinite-horizon MDPs with discounted rewards, achieving a sample complexity bound of $\tilde{O}({\frac{SA}{\epsilon^2(1-\gamma)^7}})$.
-----
-----
1421
This paper addresses the challenges of learning from multimodal data by introducing a new model that factorizes representations into multimodal discriminative and modality-specific generative factors. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on six datasets and offers flexible generative capabilities.

1858
This research proposes a universal objective for learning to sample from target distributions using the acceptance rate of the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm, unifying MCMC, GANs, and variational inference methods.
-----
-----
2325
This research proposes two methods to enhance recommender systems using side information: Trace-norm regression (TNR) and Stable Trace-norm Analysis (StaTNA).

-----
852
Researchers developed a new approach to image captioning using a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (GAN), incorporating a context-aware LSTM captioner and co-attentive discriminator for improved semantic alignment between images and captions.

-----
2289
Researchers explore the connection between Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs) by showing that an optimal transport GAN with entropy regularization can be viewed as a model maximizing a lower-bound on sample likelihoods, similar to VAEs.
-----
-----
2288
Researchers explore the connection between Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs), finding that an optimal transport GAN with entropy regularization can be viewed as a generative model that maximizes a lower-bound on sample likelihoods.
-----
-----
2439
Researchers proposed Merged-Averaged Classifiers via Hashing (MACH), a $K$-classification algorithm suitable for large $K$. MACH requires less memory ($O(d\log{K})$) and inference cost ($O(K\log{K} + d\log{K})$) compared to traditional methods.
-----
-----
569
This paper addresses the issue of mode collapse in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) by designing discriminators with strong distinguishing power to learn distributions in Wasserstein distance or KL-divergence with polynomial sample complexity.
-----
-----
1810
This research paper reinterprets Normalising Flows (NFs) through the lens of Variational Autoencoders (VAEs), introducing a new model called the Variational Denoising Autoencoder (VDAE) that generalises NFs and reveals connections to denoising autoencoders.

2722
Researchers applied deep neural networks (DNNs) to three cybersecurity tasks: Android malware classification, incident detection, and fraud detection, achieving better results compared to classical machine learning algorithms.

-----
2524
Researchers propose a new regularization scheme, MMR-Universal, for ReLU networks that provides provable robustness against various types of perturbations, including $l_1$ and $l_\infty$, and extends to any $l_p$-norm for $p\geq 1$.

570
Researchers address the issue of mode collapse in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) by designing discriminators with strong distinguishing power, enabling GANs to learn distributions in Wasserstein distance or KL-divergence with polynomial sample complexity.

1907
Researchers propose $\textrm{D}^2$GAN, a novel few-shot learning model that uses Generative Adversarial Networks to synthesize diverse and discriminative features for tasks with limited labeled data.

2264
Researchers propose the Skip RNN model to address challenges in training Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) on long sequences, such as slow inference and vanishing gradients, by learning to skip state updates and shortening the computational graph.

A novel algorithm, Deep Temporal Clustering (DTC), is proposed for unsupervised learning of timeseries data. It integrates dimensionality reduction and clustering into a single framework, outperforming traditional methods in various domains.

417
This research introduces Generative Latent Optimization (GLO), a framework that trains deep convolutional generators without discriminators, addressing the instability of adversarial optimization in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs).

1289
Here's a summary of the abstract in approximately 30 words:

The augment-REINFORCE-merge (ARM) estimator is proposed, providing an unbiased, low-variance, and computationally efficient method for backpropagating gradients through stochastic binary layers in discrete latent variable models.

812
Researchers implemented text classification and sentiment analysis tasks on mobile devices using Power-efficient CNN Domain Specific Accelerator (CNN-DSA) chips, achieving state-of-the-art results with low power consumption under 300mW.
